1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a structure algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 759. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 525/2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the structure space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 759 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 525. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The structure operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 133. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 5 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on structure structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 133. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 5. QED.
